Although their density and mix vary across outlets and topics, our heuristic suggests that questions are overwhelmingly taken up within the same article and usually linked to a subsequent answer-like span, most often in the journalist's narrative voice and less often through quoted speech. Interrogative contexts are densely populated with named individuals, organizations, and places, whereas publics and broad social groups are mentioned much less frequently, suggesting that interrogative discourse tends to foreground already prominent actors and places and thus exhibits strong personalization. We show how interrogative stance, textual uptake, and voice can be operationalized at corpus scale, and argue that combining computational methods with pragmatic and sociological perspectives can help account for how questioning practices structure contemporary news discourse.

</details>


### [77] [Instruction Set and Language for Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21836)
*Ezequiel Lopez-Rubio,Mario Pascual-Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IsalSR提出一种符号回归表示框架，通过两层字母表编码表达式DAG为字符串，并计算修剪后的规范字符串来消除结构冗余


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决符号回归中的结构冗余问题：同一表达式DAG存在多种节点编号方案，每种都占据搜索空间不同位置，消耗适应度评估却不增加多样性

Method: 提出IsalSR表示框架，将表达式DAG编码为紧凑两层字母表的字符串，计算修剪后的规范字符串作为完全标记DAG同构不变量，将所有等价表示折叠为单一规范形式

Result: 未在摘要中明确说明具体实验结果，但提出了解决结构冗余问题的技术方案

Conclusion: IsalSR框架通过规范表示消除符号回归中的结构冗余，提高搜索效率

Abstract: A fundamental but largely unaddressed obstacle in Symbolic regression (SR) is structural redundancy: every expression DAG with admits many distinct node-numbering schemes that all encode the same expression, each occupying a separate point in the search space and consuming fitness evaluations without adding diversity. We present IsalSR (Instruction Set and Language for Symbolic Regression), a representation framework that encodes expression DAGs as strings over a compact two-tier alphabet and computes a pruned canonical string -- a complete labeled-DAG isomorphism invariant -- that collapses all the equivalent representations into a single canonical form.

</details>


### [78] [Select, Label, Evaluate: Active Testing in NLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21840)
*Antonio Purificato,Maria Sofia Bucarelli,Andrea Bacciu,Amin Mantrach,Fabrizio Silvestri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Active Testing框架通过选择最具信息量的测试样本进行标注，在NLP任务中实现高达95%的标注量减少，性能估计误差在1%以内


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLP中人工标注成本和时间是主要瓶颈，特别是测试数据标注要求高质量标签且成本高昂。传统方法需要标注整个测试集，资源消耗大

Method: 1. 形式化NLP中的Active Testing框架；2. 在18个数据集和4种嵌入策略上对现有方法进行广泛基准测试；3. 引入自适应停止准则，自动确定最优样本数量

Result: 实验显示标注量减少高达95%，性能估计与完整测试集的差异在1%以内。方法效果随数据特征和任务类型变化，无单一最优方法

Conclusion: Active Testing能显著降低NLP测试标注成本，自适应停止准则解决了预定义标注预算的限制。方法选择应考虑具体任务和数据特性

Abstract: Human annotation cost and time remain significant bottlenecks in Natural Language Processing (NLP), with test data annotation being particularly expensive due to the stringent requirement for low-error and high-quality labels necessary for reliable model evaluation. Traditional approaches require annotating entire test sets, leading to substantial resource requirements. Active Testing is a framework that selects the most informative test samples for annotation. Given a labeling budget, it aims to choose the subset that best estimates model performance while minimizing cost and human effort. In this work, we formalize Active Testing in NLP and we conduct an extensive benchmarking of existing approaches across 18 datasets and 4 embedding strategies spanning 4 different NLP tasks. The experiments show annotation reductions of up to 95%, with performance estimation accuracy difference from the full test set within 1%. Our analysis reveals variations in method effectiveness across different data characteristics and task types, with no single approach emerging as universally superior. Lastly, to address the limitation of requiring a predefined annotation budget in existing sample selection strategies, we introduce an adaptive stopping criterion that automatically determines the optimal number of samples.

</details>


### [79] [Riding Brainwaves in LLM Space: Understanding Activation Patterns Using Individual Neural Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21847)
*Ajan Subramanian,Sumukh Bettadapura,Rohan Sathish*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 冻结的大型语言模型（Qwen 2.5 7B）的深层隐藏状态包含稳定、个体特异性的神经方向，这些方向能够预测个人脑电图信号，为脑电图驱动的个性化提供了几何基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着消费级脑电图设备进入日常设备（如耳塞、头带），研究语言模型能否适应个体神经响应变得重要。本文测试冻结LLM表征是否编码个体特异性的脑电图信号，即预测一个人而非另一个人的脑活动方向。

Method: 使用ZuCo语料库中30名参与者阅读自然语句时的词级脑电图数据。为每个参与者训练单独的线性探针，将冻结Qwen 2.5 7B的隐藏状态映射到该个体的脑电图功率。比较个体特异性探针与单一群体探针的性能，并进行负对照（注视计数）和混淆控制。

Result: 个体特异性探针在所有脑电图特征上均优于群体探针：对于高伽马功率，个体特异性探针达到rho=0.183，比群体探针（rho=0.020）提高9倍。个体方向具有时间稳定性（分半余弦=0.824），在个体间不可迁移（自身rho=0.369 vs. 他人rho=0.143），且与共享群体信号不同。个体特异性信号集中在模型的深层（28层中的第24层达到峰值）。结果在不同架构（LLaMA 3.1 8B）和混淆控制下保持一致。

Conclusion: 冻结语言模型的深层包含稳定、个体特异性的神经方向，为脑电图驱动的个性化提供了几何基础。这表明语言模型可以适应个体神经响应，支持消费级脑电图设备在个性化应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Consumer-grade EEG is entering everyday devices, from earbuds to headbands, raising the question of whether language models can be adapted to individual neural responses. We test this by asking whether frozen LLM representations encode person-specific EEG signals, directions in activation space that predict one person's brain activity but not another's. Using word-level EEG from 30 participants reading naturalistic sentences (ZuCo corpus), we train a separate linear probe for each person, mapping hidden states from a frozen Qwen 2.5 7B to that individual's EEG power. Person-specific probes outperform a single population probe on every EEG feature tested; for high-gamma power, the person-specific probe achieves rho = 0.183, a ninefold improvement over the population probe (rho = 0.020, p < 10^-4). A negative control, fixation count, shows no person-specific advantage (p = 0.360); fixation count reflects word length and frequency rather than individual cognition. The individual directions are temporally stable (split-half cosine = 0.824), non-transferable across people (self rho = 0.369 vs. other rho = 0.143, p < 10^-19), and distinct from the shared population signal: person-specific probes retain predictive power after the population component is removed. The person-specific signal concentrates in the model's deep layers, rising consistently with depth and peaking at Layer 24 of 28. The results are consistent across architectures (LLaMA 3.1 8B) and survive word-level confound controls. Frozen language models contain stable, person-specific neural directions in their deep layers, providing a geometric foundation for EEG-driven personalization.

</details>


### [80] [Ara-Best-RQ: Multi Dialectal Arabic SSL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21900)
*Haroun Elleuch,Ryan Whetten,Salima Mdhaffar,Yannick Estève,Fethi Bougares*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Ara-BEST-RQ：针对多方言阿拉伯语语音处理的自监督学习模型家族，在方言识别和语音识别任务上取得SOTA性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对阿拉伯语多方言语音处理缺乏专门的自监督学习模型，现有模型要么是多语言的（非专门针对阿拉伯语），要么是基于非阿拉伯语数据的单语模型，无法充分利用阿拉伯语方言特性

Method: 基于BEST-RQ架构，使用5,640小时爬取的Creative Commons语音数据结合公开数据集，预训练参数达6亿的conformer-based模型，专门针对阿拉伯语方言家族进行目标预训练

Result: 在方言识别任务上达到最先进性能，且参数量少于竞争模型；相比多语言或基于非阿拉伯语数据的单语模型，针对阿拉伯语方言的预训练显著提升下游任务性能

Conclusion: 针对特定语言家族（阿拉伯语方言）的专门预训练比通用多语言模型更有效；模型、代码和预处理数据集将公开以支持阿拉伯语语音技术的可重复性和进一步研究

Abstract: We present Ara-BEST-RQ, a family of self-supervised learning (SSL) models specifically designed for multi-dialectal Arabic speech processing. Leveraging 5,640 hours of crawled Creative Commons speech and combining it with publicly available datasets, we pre-train conformer-based BEST-RQ models up to 600M parameters. Our models are evaluated on dialect identification (DID) and automatic speech recognition (ASR) tasks, achieving state-of-the-art performance on the former while using fewer parameters than competing models. We demonstrate that family-targeted pre-training on Arabic dialects significantly improves downstream performance compared to multilingual or monolingual models trained on non-Arabic data. All models, code, and pre-processed datasets will be publicly released to support reproducibility and further research in Arabic speech technologies.

</details>


### [81] [Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning for Medical Text Summarization: A Comparative Study of Lora, Prompt Tuning, and Full Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21970)
*Ulugbek Shernazarov,Rostislav Svitsov,Bin Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LoRA在医学文本摘要任务上优于全参数微调，仅需0.6%可训练参数即可获得更高ROUGE分数


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域特定任务（如医学文本摘要）的全参数微调需要大量计算资源，参数高效微调方法通过仅更新少量参数提供有前景的替代方案

Method: 在PubMed医学摘要数据集上，比较LoRA、Prompt Tuning和Full Fine-Tuning三种方法在Flan-T5模型家族上的表现，通过多次随机种子实验和敏感性分析

Result: LoRA在Flan-T5-Large上获得43.52±0.18 ROUGE-1分数，优于全参数微调的40.67±0.21，仅需0.6%可训练参数；低秩约束提供有益的正则化效果

Conclusion: LoRA的低秩约束提供了有益的正则化，挑战了全参数更新的必要性假设，为医学文本摘要任务提供了高效且性能优越的微调方案

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models for domain-specific tasks such as medical text summarization demands substantial computational resources. Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods offer promising alternatives by updating only a small fraction of parameters. This paper compares three adaptation approaches-Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), Prompt Tuning, and Full Fine-Tuning-across the Flan-T5 model family on the PubMed medical summarization dataset. Through experiments with multiple random seeds, we demonstrate that LoRA consistently outperforms full fine-tuning, achieving 43.52 +/- 0.18 ROUGE-1 on Flan-T5-Large with only 0.6% trainable parameters compared to 40.67 +/- 0.21 for full fine-tuning. Sensitivity analyses examine the impact of LoRA rank and prompt token count. Our findings suggest the low-rank constraint provides beneficial regularization, challenging assumptions about the necessity of full parameter updates. Code is available at https://github.com/eracoding/llm-medical-summarization

</details>


### [82] [Retrieving Climate Change Disinformation by Narrative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22015)
*Max Upravitelev,Veronika Solopova,Charlott Jakob,Premtim Sahitaj,Vera Schmitt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 将气候虚假信息叙事检测重构为检索任务，提出SpecFi框架通过生成假设文档连接抽象叙事描述与具体文本实例，在无预定义标签的情况下实现叙事检测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于固定分类法的气候虚假信息叙事检测方法无法适应新兴叙事，需要一种无需预定义标签集且能灵活处理新兴叙事的检测框架

Method: 将叙事检测重构为检索任务：以叙事核心信息作为查询，从语料库中检索与之对齐的文本。提出SpecFi框架，利用基于图的社区检测生成的社区摘要作为少样本示例，生成假设文档来弥合抽象叙事描述与具体文本实例之间的差距

Result: SpecFi在CARDS数据集上达到0.505的MAP，无需访问叙事标签。引入叙事方差作为难度度量，分析显示标准检索方法在高方差叙事上性能显著下降（BM25损失63.4% MAP），而SpecFi-CS保持相对稳健（损失32.7%）。无监督社区摘要与专家构建的分类法描述接近

Conclusion: 将叙事检测重构为检索任务是可行的，SpecFi框架能有效处理新兴叙事且无需预定义标签。基于图的方法能从无标签文本中提取叙事结构，为气候虚假信息检测提供了灵活且可扩展的解决方案

Abstract: Detecting climate disinformation narratives typically relies on fixed taxonomies, which do not accommodate emerging narratives. Thus, we re-frame narrative detection as a retrieval task: given a narrative's core message as a query, rank texts from a corpus by alignment with that narrative. This formulation requires no predefined label set and can accommodate emerging narratives. We repurpose three climate disinformation datasets (CARDS, Climate Obstruction, climate change subset of PolyNarrative) for retrieval evaluation and propose SpecFi, a framework that generates hypothetical documents to bridge the gap between abstract narrative descriptions and their concrete textual instantiations. SpecFi uses community summaries from graph-based community detection as few-shot examples for generation, achieving a MAP of 0.505 on CARDS without access to narrative labels. We further introduce narrative variance, an embedding-based difficulty metric, and show via partial correlation analysis that standard retrieval degrades on high-variance narratives (BM25 loses 63.4% of MAP), while SpecFi-CS remains robust (32.7% loss). Our analysis also reveals that unsupervised community summaries converge on descriptions close to expert-crafted taxonomies, suggesting that graph-based methods can surface narrative structure from unlabeled text.

</details>


### [83] [Dual-Space Knowledge Distillation with Key-Query Matching for Large Language Models with Vocabulary Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22056)
*Stella Eva Tsiapali,Cong-Thanh Do,Kate Knill*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了DSKD-CMA方法的注意力机制，揭示了其优缺点，并提出了基于生成对抗学习的改进方法DSKD-CMA-GA，在文本生成质量上取得了稳定提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽然性能优越但部署成本高，知识蒸馏可以训练更小的学生模型来模仿教师模型以提高效率。DSKD-CMA是跨不同分词器LLM知识蒸馏的SOTA方法，但其内部工作机制不够透明，且存在键查询分布不匹配的问题

Method: 通过手动分词对齐探测和热力图可视化系统分析DSKD-CMA的注意力机制，在此基础上提出基于生成对抗学习的新方法DSKD-CMA-GA，解决不同模型计算的键和查询之间的分布不匹配问题

Result: 实验显示在文本生成质量上获得了适度但一致的ROUGE-L提升，特别是在分布外数据上平均提升+0.37，缩小了跨分词器和同分词器知识蒸馏之间的性能差距

Conclusion: DSKD-CMA-GA通过生成对抗学习有效解决了跨模型知识蒸馏中的分布不匹配问题，在保持模型效率的同时提升了文本生成质量，特别是在分布外场景下表现更优

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance across language tasks, but are costly to deploy due to their size and resource demands. Knowledge Distillation (KD) addresses this by training smaller Student models to mimic larger Teacher models, improving efficiency without significant performance loss. Dual-Space Knowledge Distillation with Cross-Model Attention (DSKD-CMA) has emerged as a SOTA method for KD between LLMs with distinct tokenizers, yet its internal workings remain largely opaque. In this work, we systematically analyse the attention mechanism of DSKD-CMA through manual token alignment probing and heatmap visualisations, revealing both strengths and limitations. Building on this, we introduce a novel method, DSKD-CMA-GA, based on Generative Adversarial (GA) learning, to address the mismatched distributions between the keys and queries computed from distinct models. Experiments show modest but consistent ROUGE-L gains in text generation quality, particularly on out-of-distribution data (+0.37 on average), narrowing the gap between cross- and same-tokenizer KD.

</details>


### [84] [Autoregressive vs. Masked Diffusion Language Models: A Controlled Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22075)
*Caio Vicentino*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AR与MDLM语言模型在相同条件下的对比研究：两者训练效率相当，AR收敛更快但易过拟合，MDLM收敛慢但持续改进；AR生成流畅但重复，MDLM生成更多样但偶有语法问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在相同数据、计算预算和硬件条件下，系统比较自回归（AR）和掩码扩散（MDLM）语言模型的性能差异，隔离生成范式作为唯一变量，为模型选择提供实证依据。

Method: 使用相同数据（TinyStories的5000万token）、相同计算预算（2万步，批量大小32，序列长度512）和相同硬件（NVIDIA H100 80GB）训练AR和MDLM模型，进行受控实验设计。

Result: 1. 训练吞吐量相当（约5万token/秒），MDLM仅多4.7%时间；2. AR在第14000步收敛并开始过拟合，MDLM收敛更慢且在第20000步仍在改进；3. AR生成流畅但重复（99.8%以相同词开头），MDLM生成更多样（93.4%独特5词开头，更高Distinct-n，更低Self-BLEU）但偶有语法问题。

Conclusion: AR和MDLM在相同条件下表现出不同的训练动态和生成特性：AR收敛快但易过拟合，生成流畅但重复；MDLM收敛慢但持续改进，生成更多样但语法一致性稍差。两种范式需要不同的计算最优训练策略，选择取决于应用需求。

Abstract: We present a controlled empirical comparison between autoregressive (AR) and masked diffusion (MDLM) language models. Both models are trained on identical data (50M tokens from TinyStories), identical compute budget (20,000 steps, batch size 32, sequence length 512), and identical hardware (NVIDIA H100 80GB), isolating the generation paradigm as the sole variable. We report three findings. First, both paradigms achieve comparable training throughput (~50K tokens/second), with MDLM requiring only 4.7% more wall-clock time. Second, AR converges faster and begins overfitting by step 14,000, while MDLM converges more slowly and is still improving at step 20,000, suggesting different compute-optimal training regimes. Third, quantitative diversity analysis over 1,000 generated samples reveals a structural diversity-fluency trade-off: AR produces fluent but repetitive outputs (99.8% begin with the same word), while MDLM generates more diverse narratives (93.4% unique 5-word openings, higher Distinct-n, lower Self-BLEU), at the cost of occasional grammatical inconsistencies. All code, trained checkpoints, and data pipelines are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [85] [Multiperspectivity as a Resource for Narrative Similarity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22103)
*Max Upravitelev,Veronika Solopova,Jing Yang,Charlott Jakob,Premtim Sahitaj,Ariana Sahitaj,Vera Schmitt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出通过集成多个LLM角色来应对叙事相似性评估中的多视角挑战，在SemEval-2026 Task 4数据集上达到0.705准确率，发现实践者角色虽个体表现较差但误差相关性低，集成增益更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叙事相似性预测本质上是解释性任务，同一文本的不同有效解读会产生不同的相似性判断，这对编码单一真实标签的语义评估基准构成根本挑战。研究旨在将这种多视角性纳入预测系统的决策过程，而非将其视为需要克服的问题。

Method: 创建了31个LLM角色组成的集成系统，角色范围包括遵循解释框架的实践者和更直观的普通风格角色。在SemEval-2026 Task 4数据集上进行实验，采用多数投票机制，分析集成规模、角色类型对性能的影响。

Result: 系统达到0.705的准确率。准确率随集成规模增加而提高，符合弱化独立性条件下的孔多塞陪审团定理动态。实践者角色个体表现较差但误差相关性更低，在多数投票下产生更大的集成增益。错误分析显示，所有角色类别中，性别聚焦的解释词汇与准确率呈一致的负相关。

Conclusion: 研究证明了将多视角解释纳入预测系统的可行性，实践者角色虽然个体性能较差但能提供多样化视角。性别聚焦词汇的负相关表明这些维度可能与基准无关，或代表基准中缺失的有效解释。这强调了需要能够容纳解释多样性的评估框架。

Abstract: Predicting narrative similarity can be understood as an inherently interpretive task: different, equally valid readings of the same text can produce divergent interpretations and thus different similarity judgments, posing a fundamental challenge for semantic evaluation benchmarks that encode a single ground truth. Rather than treating this multiperspectivity as a challenge to overcome, we propose to incorporate it in the decision making process of predictive systems. To explore this strategy, we created an ensemble of 31 LLM personas. These range from practitioners following interpretive frameworks to more intuitive, lay-style characters. Our experiments were conducted on the SemEval-2026 Task 4 dataset, where the system achieved an accuracy score of 0.705. Accuracy improves with ensemble size, consistent with Condorcet Jury Theorem-like dynamics under weakened independence. Practitioner personas perform worse individually but produce less correlated errors, yielding larger ensemble gains under majority voting. Our error analysis reveals a consistent negative association between gender-focused interpretive vocabulary and accuracy across all persona categories, suggesting either attention to dimensions not relevant for the benchmark or valid interpretations absent from the ground truth. This finding underscores the need for evaluation frameworks that account for interpretive plurality.

</details>


### [86] [The Semantic Ladder: A Framework for Progressive Formalization of Natural Language Content for Knowledge Graphs and AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22136)
*Lars Vogt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"语义阶梯"架构框架，通过渐进式形式化解决自然语言与形式语义模型之间的鸿沟，支持从文本片段到本体论和逻辑模型的语义层次转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义数据和知识基础设施需要调和自然语言（知识创建和传播的主要形式）与形式语义模型（支持机器可操作的集成、互操作和推理）之间的根本差异。当数据录入点需要完全语义形式化时，这一挑战尤为突出。

Method: 引入"语义阶梯"架构框架，基于模块化语义单元作为可识别意义载体，组织从自然语言文本片段到本体论和更高阶逻辑模型的不同语义明确性层次的表示。层级间的转换支持语义丰富化、语句结构化和逻辑建模，同时保持语义连续性和可追溯性。

Result: 该框架支持增量构建语义知识空间，减少语义解析负担，并支持异构表示（包括自然语言、结构化语义模型和基于向量的嵌入）的集成。为可扩展、可互操作和AI就绪的数据和知识基础设施提供基础。

Conclusion: 语义阶梯通过渐进式形式化方法，为解决自然语言与形式语义模型之间的鸿沟提供了系统化框架，为构建下一代语义基础设施奠定基础。

Abstract: Semantic data and knowledge infrastructures must reconcile two fundamentally different forms of representation: natural language, in which most knowledge is created and communicated, and formal semantic models, which enable machine-actionable integration, interoperability, and reasoning. Bridging this gap remains a central challenge, particularly when full semantic formalization is required at the point of data entry. Here, we introduce the Semantic Ladder, an architectural framework that enables the progressive formalization of data and knowledge. Building on the concept of modular semantic units as identifiable carriers of meaning, the framework organizes representations across levels of increasing semantic explicitness, ranging from natural language text snippets to ontology-based and higher-order logical models. Transformations between levels support semantic enrichment, statement structuring, and logical modelling while preserving semantic continuity and traceability. This approach enables the incremental construction of semantic knowledge spaces, reduces the semantic parsing burden, and supports the integration of heterogeneous representations, including natural language, structured semantic models, and vector-based embeddings. The Semantic Ladder thereby provides a foundation for scalable, interoperable, and AI-ready data and knowledge infrastructures.

</details>


### [87] [Enhancing Document-Level Machine Translation via Filtered Synthetic Corpora and Two-Stage LLM Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22186)
*Ireh Kim,Tesia Sker,Chanwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出两阶段微调策略，利用LLM增强文档级数据解决文档级机器翻译中数据稀缺和幻觉问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在机器翻译中表现不如传统编码器-解码器系统，但擅长建模上下文信息，适合文档级翻译。然而文档级MT面临两个关键挑战：1) 大规模高质量文档级平行数据稀缺；2) LLM在生成时容易产生幻觉和遗漏

Method: 提出两阶段微调策略：首先通过LLM将摘要数据转换为文档级平行数据，并使用sacreBLEU、COMET和LaBSE余弦相似度等多指标过滤以提高数据质量；然后采用两阶段微调：先在丰富的句子级MT资源上微调，再在过滤后的文档级语料上微调

Result: 论文未在摘要中提供具体实验结果，但提出的方法旨在解决文档级机器翻译中的数据稀缺和质量问题

Conclusion: 通过LLM增强文档级数据和两阶段微调策略，可以解决文档级机器翻译中数据稀缺和LLM幻觉问题，提升LLM在文档级翻译任务中的性能

Abstract: In Machine Translation, Large Language Models (LLMs) have generally underperformed compared to conventional encoder-decoder systems and thus see limited adoption. However, LLMs excel at modeling contextual information, making them a natural fit for document-level translation tasks where coherence across sentences is crucial. Despite this potential, document-level MT with LLMs faces two key challenges: (1) the scarcity of large-scale, high-quality document-level parallel data; and (2) the propensity of LLMs to introduce hallucinations and omissions during generation. To address these challenges, we propose a two-stage fine-tuning strategy leveraging LLM-augmented document-level data. First, we augment data by converting summarization data into document-level parallel data using a LLM, and then filter it using multiple metrics, leveraging sacreBLEU, COMET, and LaBSE-based cosine similarity-to improve data quality. Finally, we employ a two-stage fine-tuning strategy: first fine-tuning on the abundant sentence-level MT resources, and then on the filtered document-level corpus.

</details>


### [88] [Gumbel Distillation for Parallel Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22216)
*Chi Zhang,Xixi Hu,Bo Liu,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Gumbel蒸馏方法，通过Gumbel-Max技巧创建确定性映射，让并行解码器学习自回归教师模型的token序列联合分布，显著提升并行语言模型的生成质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归语言模型的顺序解码速度慢，而并行解码方法虽然速度快但生成质量差，难以建模token序列的复杂联合分布。需要缩小并行解码器与自回归模型之间的性能差距。

Method: 提出Gumbel蒸馏技术，利用Gumbel-Max技巧从潜在Gumbel噪声空间到高性能自回归教师模型输出token创建确定性映射。该方法与模型无关，可无缝集成到MDLM、BD3-LM等多种并行解码架构中。

Result: 在LM1B和OpenWebText数据集上的实验表明，Gumbel蒸馏显著提升了并行语言模型的生成质量：在OpenWebText数据集上训练的MDLM模型，MAUVE分数提高了30.0%，生成困惑度降低了10.5%。

Conclusion: Gumbel蒸馏是一种有效的蒸馏技术，能够帮助并行解码器学习token序列的复杂联合分布，显著缩小与自回归教师模型的性能差距，同时保持并行解码的速度优势。

Abstract: The slow, sequential nature of autoregressive (AR) language models has driven the adoption of parallel decoding methods. However, these non-AR models often sacrifice generation quality as they struggle to model the complex joint distribution of token sequences. To narrow this performance gap, we introduce Gumbel Distillation, a novel distillation technique that enables parallel decoders to learn this distribution effectively. Our method leverages the Gumbel-Max trick to create a deterministic mapping from a latent Gumbel noise space to the output tokens of a high-performing AR teacher. As a model-agnostic technique, Gumbel Distillation seamlessly integrates with diverse parallel decoding architectures, including MDLM and BD3-LM. Experiments on LM1B and OpenWebText show that Gumbel Distillation substantially improves the generation quality of parallel language models, achieving a 30.0% improvement in MAUVE score and 10.5% in generative perplexity over MDLM trained on OpenWebText dataset. Code available at https://github.com/hxixixh/gumbel-distill.

</details>


### [89] [Adapting Self-Supervised Speech Representations for Cross-lingual Dysarthria Detection in Parkinson's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22225)
*Abner Hernandez,Eunjung Yeo,Kwanghee Choi,Chin-Jou Li,Zhengjun Yue,Rohan Kumar Das,Jan Rusz,Mathew Magimai Doss,Juan Rafael Orozco-Arroyave,Tomás Arias-Vergara,Andreas Maier,Elmar Nöth,David R. Mortensen,David Harwath,Paula Andrea Perez-Toro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种表示层语言偏移方法，通过基于健康语音质心的向量适配，将源语言自监督语音表示与目标语言分布对齐，以改善跨语言构音障碍检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构音障碍语音数据有限，跨语言检测成为重要但具有挑战性的问题。主要困难在于语音表示通常编码语言依赖的结构，这会干扰构音障碍检测。

Method: 提出表示层语言偏移方法，使用基于健康对照语音估计的质心向量适配，将源语言自监督语音表示与目标语言分布对齐。

Result: 在捷克语、德语和西班牙语的帕金森病语音数据集上评估，语言偏移方法在跨语言设置中显著提高了敏感性和F1分数，在多语言设置中也获得了较小但一致的改进。表示分析显示该方法减少了嵌入空间中的语言身份信息。

Conclusion: 表示层语言偏移方法有效减少了语音表示中的语言依赖结构，改善了跨语言构音障碍检测性能，支持了该方法能够移除语言依赖结构的解释。

Abstract: The limited availability of dysarthric speech data makes cross-lingual detection an important but challenging problem. A key difficulty is that speech representations often encode language-dependent structure that can confound dysarthria detection. We propose a representation-level language shift (LS) that aligns source-language self-supervised speech representations with the target-language distribution using centroid-based vector adaptation estimated from healthy-control speech. We evaluate the approach on oral DDK recordings from Parkinson's disease speech datasets in Czech, German, and Spanish under both cross-lingual and multilingual settings. LS substantially improves sensitivity and F1 in cross-lingual settings, while yielding smaller but consistent gains in multilingual settings. Representation analysis further shows that LS reduces language identity in the embedding space, supporting the interpretation that LS removes language-dependent structure.

</details>


### [90] [MemDLM: Memory-Enhanced DLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22241)
*Zehua Pei,Hui-Ling Zhen,Weizhe Lin,Sinno Jialin Pan,Yunhe Wang,Mingxuan Yuan,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemDLM通过双层优化在训练中嵌入模拟去噪过程，使用参数化记忆捕获局部轨迹经验，减少训练-推理不匹配，提升长上下文理解和检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLMs）相比自回归模型具有并行解码和灵活生成的优势，但存在显著的训练-推理不匹配问题：DLMs使用静态单步掩码预测目标进行训练，但通过多步渐进去噪轨迹进行部署。

Method: 提出MemDLM（记忆增强DLM），通过双层优化在训练中嵌入模拟去噪过程。内层循环更新快速权重形成参数化记忆，捕获每个样本的局部轨迹经验；外层循环基于该记忆更新基础模型。将记忆压力从词元表示卸载到参数上。

Result: MemDLM实现更快的收敛速度和更低的训练损失。推理时重新启用内层循环作为适应步骤，在长上下文理解上获得额外增益。参数化记忆在推理时表现为一种新兴的权重内检索机制，在Needle-in-a-Haystack检索任务上进一步减少词元级注意力瓶颈。

Conclusion: MemDLM通过参数化记忆机制有效缓解了扩散语言模型的训练-推理不匹配问题，提升了模型性能和长上下文处理能力，为DLMs的实际部署提供了改进方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) offer attractive advantages over Auto-Regressive (AR) models, such as full-attention parallel decoding and flexible generation. However, they suffer from a notable train-inference mismatch: DLMs are trained with a static, single-step masked prediction objective, but deployed through a multi-step progressive denoising trajectory. We propose MemDLM (Memory-Enhanced DLM), which narrows this gap by embedding a simulated denoising process into training via Bi-level Optimization. An inner loop updates a set of fast weights, forming a Parametric Memory that captures the local trajectory experience of each sample, while an outer loop updates the base model conditioned on this memory. By offloading memorization pressure from token representations to parameters, MemDLM yields faster convergence and lower training loss. Moreover, the inner loop can be re-enabled at inference time as an adaptation step, yielding additional gains on long-context understanding. We find that, when activated at inference time, this Parametric Memory acts as an emergent in-weight retrieval mechanism, helping MemDLM further reduce token-level attention bottlenecks on challenging Needle-in-a-Haystack retrieval tasks. Code: https://github.com/JarvisPei/MemDLM.

</details>


### [91] [Greater accessibility can amplify discrimination in generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22260)
*Carolin Holtermann,Minh Duc Bui,Kaitlyn Zhou,Valentin Hofmann,Katharina von der Wense,Anne Lauscher*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 音频大语言模型存在系统性性别歧视，仅根据说话者声音就向性别刻板印象的形容词和职业倾斜，且比文本交互中的偏见更严重。语音界面不仅扩展了文本模型，还引入了与副语言线索相关的独特偏见机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管数百万人依赖大语言模型进行教育、工作和医疗，但这些模型会复制和放大训练数据中的社会偏见。文本界面对许多用户（如识字有限、运动障碍或仅使用移动设备的用户）构成障碍。语音交互有望提高可访问性，但语音携带的身份线索用户难以掩盖，这引发了可访问性提升是否以公平对待为代价的担忧。

Method: 研究音频大语言模型的性别歧视现象，通过实验展示仅基于说话者声音就产生的系统性偏见。进行补充调查（n=1000）了解用户对属性推断的态度。演示音高操纵作为潜在缓解策略，系统性地调节性别歧视输出。

Result: 音频大语言模型表现出系统性性别歧视，响应向性别刻板印象的形容词和职业倾斜，且偏见程度超过文本交互。调查显示不常使用聊天机器人的用户最不愿接受未公开的属性推断，并在发现此类做法时最可能停止使用。音高操纵可以系统性地调节性别歧视输出。

Conclusion: 语音界面在扩展可访问性的同时，创造了新的歧视途径，公平性和可访问性必须同时解决。研究揭示了AI发展的关键矛盾：通过语音界面扩大可访问性的努力同时为歧视创造了新途径。

Abstract: Hundreds of millions of people rely on large language models (LLMs) for education, work, and even healthcare. Yet these models are known to reproduce and amplify social biases present in their training data. Moreover, text-based interfaces remain a barrier for many, for example, users with limited literacy, motor impairments, or mobile-only devices. Voice interaction promises to expand accessibility, but unlike text, speech carries identity cues that users cannot easily mask, raising concerns about whether accessibility gains may come at the cost of equitable treatment. Here we show that audio-enabled LLMs exhibit systematic gender discrimination, shifting responses toward gender-stereotyped adjectives and occupations solely on the basis of speaker voice, and amplifying bias beyond that observed in text-based interaction. Thus, voice interfaces do not merely extend text models to a new modality but introduce distinct bias mechanisms tied to paralinguistic cues. Complementary survey evidence ($n=1,000$) shows that infrequent chatbot users are most hesitant to undisclosed attribute inference and most likely to disengage when such practices are revealed. To demonstrate a potential mitigation strategy, we show that pitch manipulation can systematically regulate gender-discriminatory outputs. Overall, our findings reveal a critical tension in AI development: efforts to expand accessibility through voice interfaces simultaneously create new pathways for discrimination, demanding that fairness and accessibility be addressed in tandem.

</details>


### [92] [TiCo: Time-Controllable Training for Spoken Dialogue Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22267)
*Kai-Wei Chang,Wei-Chih Chen,En-Pei Hu,Hung-yi Lee,James Glass*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TiCo是一种简单的后训练方法，使语音对话模型能够遵循时间约束指令并生成可控时长的响应，通过口语时间标记帮助模型在生成过程中估计已用时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音对话模型虽然能生成自然的语音响应，但缺乏时间意识，难以遵循时长相关指令（如"生成约15秒的响应"）。这在语音助手等实际应用中很重要，因为控制响应时长可以提升交互质量。

Method: TiCo通过口语时间标记（如<10.6秒>）使模型在生成过程中估计已用说话时间，帮助模型保持时间意识并调整剩余内容以满足目标时长。该方法简单高效，仅需少量数据且无需额外问答对，依赖自生成和强化学习。

Result: 实验结果表明，TiCo显著提高了模型对时长约束的遵循度，同时保持了响应质量。对开源和商业语音对话模型的实证评估显示，TiCo有效解决了现有模型在时间控制需求方面的不足。

Conclusion: TiCo为语音对话模型提供了简单有效的后训练方法，使其能够遵循时间约束指令并生成可控时长的响应，这对于提升语音交互系统的实用性和用户体验具有重要意义。

Abstract: We propose TiCo, a simple post-training method for enabling spoken dialogue models (SDMs) to follow time-constrained instructions and generate responses with controllable duration. This capability is valuable for real-world spoken language systems such as voice assistants and interactive agents, where controlling response duration can improve interaction quality. However, despite their strong ability to generate natural spoken responses, existing models lack time awareness and struggle to follow duration-related instructions (e.g., "Please generate a response lasting about 15 seconds"). Through an empirical evaluation of both open-source and commercial SDMs, we show that they frequently fail to satisfy such time-control requirements. TiCo addresses this limitation by enabling models to estimate elapsed speaking time during generation through Spoken Time Markers (STM) (e.g., <10.6 seconds>). These markers help the model maintain awareness of time and adjust the remaining content to meet the target duration. TiCo is simple and efficient: it requires only a small amount of data and no additional question-answer pairs, relying instead on self-generation and reinforcement learning. Experimental results show that TiCo significantly improves adherence to duration constraints while preserving response quality.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [Fast Solving Complete 2000-Node Optimization Using Stochastic-Computing Simulated Annealing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20197)
*Kota Katsuki,Duckgyu Shin,Naoya Onizawa,Takahiro Hanyu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于随机计算的模拟退火算法(SC-SA)，用于解决大规模组合优化问题，相比传统SA实现了几个数量级的加速，并在MAX-CUT问题上取得了更好的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模拟退火算法在解决大规模组合优化问题时收敛速度较慢，需要更高效的算法来处理大规模问题如MAX-CUT。

Method: 采用随机计算技术设计SC-SA算法，利用随机比特流进行计算，能够快速收敛到问题的全局最小能量状态。

Result: SC-SA相比典型SA实现了几个数量级的加速；在K2000（2000节点完整优化问题）上取得了比其他现有方法更好的MAX-CUT分数；在Gset基准测试中表现优异。

Conclusion: 基于随机计算的模拟退火算法(SC-SA)能够有效解决大规模组合优化问题，在收敛速度和求解质量方面均优于传统方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we evaluate stochastic-computing simulated annealing (SC-SA) for solving large-scale combinatorial optimization problems. SC-SA is designed using stochastic computing, where the computatoin is reazlied using random bitstream, resulting in fast converging to the global minimum energy of the problems. The proposed SC-SA is compared with a typical SA and existing simulated-annealing (SA) processors on the maximum cut (MAX-CUT) problems, such as Gset that is a benchmark for SA. The simulation results show that SC-SA realizes a few orders of magnitude faster than a typical SA. In addition, SC-SA achieves better MAX-CUT scores than other existing methods on K2000 that is a complete 2000-node optimization problem.

</details>


### [94] [The Coercive Projection Theorem for Canonical Reciprocal Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20205)
*Jonathan Washburn,Amir Rahnamai Barghi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出有限数据框架，在可分互易成本下认证正向量零缺陷配置，构建在可识别性轨迹上局部最大的规范决策程序


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个有限数据框架来认证正向量在规范可分互易成本下的零缺陷配置，解决在守恒约束和短窗口观测下的决策问题

Method: 基于识别组合律和局部二次校准推导标量成本特征，在守恒约束和有理信号类短窗口观测下构建规范决策程序Φ*=A∘B∘P，其中投影/强制核心由规范成本公理强制，聚合/重构步骤在非退化可识别性轨迹上指定

Result: 证明该标量成本在非恒定连续成本中由识别组合律和平衡点的局部二次校准特征化，构建的规范决策程序在所有可靠程序中在可识别性轨迹上局部最大

Conclusion: 建立了有限数据下零缺陷配置认证的理论框架，规范决策程序在可识别性轨迹上具有局部最优性，为可分互易成本下的配置认证提供了系统方法

Abstract: We develop a finite-data framework for certifying \emph{zero-defect} (neutral) configurations of positive vectors under the canonical separable reciprocal cost. We show that this scalar cost is characterized among non-constant continuous costs by the Recognition Composition Law together with a local quadratic calibration at balance; in particular, reciprocity symmetry and the normalization at the neutral point follow from the composition law. Under a conservation constraint and short-window observations of a rational (finite--state) signal class, we construct a canonical decision procedure that is \emph{locally maximal on the identifiability locus} among all sound procedures: any sound rule that resolves a datum must agree with the canonical output, and cannot resolve strictly more cases.
  The method is organized as $Φ^\ast=A\circ B\circ P$: the projection/coercivity core is forced by the canonical-cost axioms, while the aggregation/reconstruction step is specified on a non-degenerate identifiability locus (e.g.\ a Hankel invertibility condition).

</details>


### [95] [Dynamic Menu-Based Pricing for Electric Vehicle Charging with Vehicle-to-Grid Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20226)
*Mozhdeh Hematiboroujeni,Pierre Le Bodic,Adel N. Toosi,Markus Wagner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于菜单的电动汽车充放电定价机制，通过双层优化建模解决V2G激励不足问题，显著提升运营商利润和降低用户支付


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动汽车快速增长带来电网压力，现有电价机制对V2G放电激励不足，需要设计有效的定价策略来协调充电和放电行为

Method: 针对停车场运营商管理多个充电桩的场景，提出菜单式定价机制：用户申报能量需求和停车时长，运营商基于实时电价提供充放电选项菜单；将双层优化问题转化为单层模型求解

Result: 相比无V2G基线，运营商利润增加30%，用户支付降低17%；相比常用电价基线，运营商利润提升22-29%，用户支付降低9-18%，V2G贡献增加87-235%

Conclusion: 动态菜单式定价框架为实时EV充电和V2G集成提供了实用、计算高效且经济优势显著的方法

Abstract: The number of electric vehicles is rapidly increasing worldwide. This growth brings significant environmental benefits but also introduces new challenges: uncoordinated charging can place stress on the grid, particularly during peak hours. Beyond these challenges lies the opportunity for electric vehicles to feed energy back to the grid (V2G), which helps balance supply and demand and supports renewable energy. However, current pricing schemes such as time-of-use tariffs provide little incentive for discharging. To study incentive design in a realistic context, we focus on a parking lot operator who manages multiple EV chargers. We propose a menu-based pricing mechanism in which each EV declares its energy requirement and parking duration; given the retail real-time electricity prices, the operator offers a menu of options that trade off the allowed level of discharging and the associated price. We formulate this interaction as a bilevel optimization problem and reformulate it into a single-level model. Results show that, relative to a no-V2G baseline, the proposed mechanism increases operator profit by 30% and reduces EV payments by 17%. Compared to widely used tariff baselines, it improves operator profit by 22-29 percent, lowers EV payments by 9-18 percent, and increases V2G contribution by 87-235 percent. Overall, the results show that the proposed dynamic menu-based pricing framework provides a practical, computationally efficient, and economically advantageous approach for real-time EV charging and V2G integration.

</details>


### [96] [Compact Lifted Relaxations for Low-Rank Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20228)
*Ryan Cory-Wright,Jean Pauphilet*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文提出了针对秩约束二次优化问题的可处理凸松弛方法，通过提升半定规划技术，将高维约束简化为紧凑形式，适用于矩阵补全、基追踪和降秩回归等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的秩约束二次优化问题通常只在目标函数或约束具有谱结构（置换不变性）时才存在可处理的松弛方法。本文旨在为更一般的秩约束二次优化问题开发可处理的凸松弛方法，特别是那些不要求谱结构的问题。

Method: 采用提升半定规划技术，首先将问题提升到高维空间，然后证明许多矩矩阵块是冗余的，从而推导出仅包含两个半定约束的紧凑松弛：一个维度为nm+1，另一个维度为n+m。对于特定问题（矩阵补全、基追踪、降秩回归），利用额外结构进一步简化，得到维度最多为2max(n,m)的紧凑公式。

Result: 获得了可扩展的半定规划边界，适用于广泛的低秩二次问题。原始直接提升需要维度n²+nm+1的大规模半定约束，而新方法将其简化为两个更小的半定约束，显著降低了计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 本文开发了针对秩约束二次优化问题的可处理凸松弛框架，突破了传统方法对谱结构的依赖，通过紧凑的提升半定规划公式实现了可扩展的计算，为矩阵补全、基追踪和降秩回归等实际问题提供了有效的求解工具。

Abstract: We develop tractable convex relaxations for rank-constrained quadratic optimization problems over $n \times m$ matrices, a setting for which tractable relaxations are typically only available when the objective or constraints admit spectral (permutation-invariant) structure. We derive lifted semidefinite relaxations that do not require such spectral terms. Although a direct lifting introduces a large semidefinite constraint in dimension $n^2 + nm + 1$, we prove that many blocks of moment matrix are redundant and derive an equivalent compact relaxation that only involves two semidefinite constraints of dimension $nm + 1$ and $n+m$ respectively. For matrix completion, basis pursuit, and reduced-rank regression problems, we exploit additional structure to obtain even more compact formulations involving semidefinite matrices of dimension at most $2\max(n,m)$. Overall, we obtain scalable semidefinite bounds for a broad class of low-rank quadratic problems.

</details>


### [97] [Time-Reversed BSDEs for Accurate Gradient Estimation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20455)
*Yuhang Mei,Amirhossein Taghvaei*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于时间反向BSDE的梯度估计器，用于扩散模型微调，相比现有adjoint matching方法具有更稳定的梯度估计和潜在更低的方差


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于随机最优控制的扩散模型微调方法（如adjoint matching）使用的伴随过程不适用于前向扩散过滤，导致梯度估计不稳定或高方差

Method: 通过反向随机微分方程视角重新审视扩散模型梯度估计，提出基于时间反向BSDE公式的替代估计器，产生适应底层过滤的伴随过程

Result: 数值实验表明，在玩具扩散模型微调中，提出的方法改善了梯度稳定性并具有竞争性性能

Conclusion: 基于时间反向BSDE的梯度估计器为扩散模型微调提供了更稳定的梯度估计方法，有望替代adjoint matching成为新的先进方法

Abstract: There is a growing literature adopting a stochastic optimal control (SOC) perspective to fine-tune diffusion models and related generative policies. A prominent class of methods, known as iterative diffusion optimization, solves the SOC problem by simulating the diffusion process, evaluating a loss function, and applying stochastic optimization algorithms, with adjoint matching emerging as a state-of-the-art approach. However, the adjoint process used in these methods is not adapted to the forward diffusion filtration, which can lead to unstable or high-variance gradient estimates. In this paper, we revisit gradient estimation in diffusion models through the lens of backward stochastic differential equations (BSDEs). We propose an alternative estimator based on a time-reversed BSDE formulation introduced in our prior work, which produces an adjoint process adapted to the underlying filtration. This adapted structure leads to more stable gradient estimates with potentially lower variance. We analyze the accuracy of the proposed estimator and compare it with adjoint matching. Numerical experiments on fine-tuning toy diffusion models demonstrate improved gradient stability and competitive performance.

</details>


### [98] [Perturbation Duality for Robust and Distributionally Robust Optimization: Short and General Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20503)
*Louis L. Chen,Jake Roth,Johannes O. Royset*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文将扰动对偶（Fenchel-Rockafellar凸分析）应用于鲁棒优化和分布鲁棒优化，作为统一框架推导对偶表示，突破了传统方法的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒优化和分布鲁棒优化的对偶方法主要依赖鞍点论证、拉格朗日技术和锥对偶，缺乏统一框架。本文旨在展示扰动对偶作为一种更通用、统一的方法论，能够克服传统方法的局限性，特别是在处理非紧致假设和统一不同模型方面。

Method: 应用Fenchel-Rockafellar凸分析中的扰动对偶方法，首先将其应用于统一phi-散度和Wasserstein模糊集的分布鲁棒优化框架，通过最优传输和条件矩约束建立对偶表示。引入基于扰动分析的替代条件，利用可交换性原则。然后通过双函数表示统一鲁棒对偶概念，提供透明化表征。

Result: 成功建立了无需紧致假设的对偶表示，突破了先前认为必要的限制条件。开发了包含现有文献中所有公式的双函数表示，提供了简洁通用的证明，显著简化了近期结果。展示了扰动对偶在统一鲁棒对偶概念方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 扰动对偶是鲁棒优化和分布鲁棒优化中一个多功能且未充分利用的框架，为广泛模型提供了概念统一和技术通用性，能够简化证明并突破传统方法的限制。

Abstract: Duality is a foundational tool in robust and distributionally robust optimization (RO and DRO), underpinning both analytical insights and tractable reformulations. The prevailing approaches in the literature primarily rely on saddle-point arguments, Lagrangian techniques, and conic duality. In contrast, this paper applies perturbation duality in the sense of Fenchel--Rockafellar convex analysis and demonstrates its effectiveness as a general and unifying methodology for deriving dual formulations in RO and DRO. We first apply perturbation duality to a recently proposed DRO framework that unifies phi-divergence and Wasserstein ambiguity sets through optimal transport with conditional moment constraints. We establish the associated dual representation without imposing compactness assumptions previously conjectured to be necessary, instead introducing alternative conditions motivated by perturbation analysis and leveraging the Interchangeability Principle. We then revisit the concept of robust duality -- commonly described as ``primal-worst equals dual-best'' -- and show that perturbation-based formulations provide a unified and transparent characterization of this principle. In particular, we develop a bifunction-based representation that encompasses existing formulations in the literature and yields concise and general proofs, substantially simplifying recent results. This work positions perturbation duality as a versatile and underutilized framework for RO and DRO, offering both conceptual unification and technical generality across a broad class of models.

</details>


### [99] [Local Convergence Analysis of ADMM for Nonconvex Composite Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20751)
*Xiyuan Xie,Lihua Yang,Qia li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了ADMM算法在非凸复合优化问题中的局部收敛性，针对现代成像和机器学习模型中的一类特殊问题，证明了在适当条件下ADMM的局部收敛性和线性收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于现代成像和机器学习模型中出现的非凸复合优化问题，这类问题包含连续可微（可能非凸）的平滑项与线性映射复合的多面体凸非光滑项。虽然ADMM在凸优化中已有成熟理论，但在非凸问题中的收敛性分析相对缺乏，特别是Rockafellar近期工作[29,30]启发了对这类问题的深入研究。

Method: 方法包括：1）首先给出了多面体凸函数的Moreau包络的局部强凸性基本证明；2）基于此性质，在适当假设下证明了强变分充分条件成立；3）利用强变分充分条件推导ADMM迭代的下降不等式，形式类似于凸问题的经典下降分析；4）针对合适的惩罚参数选择，建立了ADMM到原始-对偶解的局部收敛性；5）当约束集为多面体凸时，证明了局部线性收敛速率；6）通过三个分析性示例说明结果的适用性和局部假设的必要性。

Result: 主要结果：1）证明了多面体凸函数的Moreau包络具有局部强凸性；2）在所考虑问题下强变分充分条件成立；3）推导了ADMM迭代的下降不等式；4）建立了ADMM到原始-对偶解的局部收敛性；5）当约束集为多面体凸时，获得了局部线性收敛速率；6）通过示例验证了理论结果的适用性，并展示了局部假设的必要性。

Conclusion: 结论：该研究为ADMM在非凸复合优化问题中的收敛性提供了理论保证，扩展了ADMM的应用范围到现代成像和机器学习模型。通过建立局部强凸性、强变分充分条件和下降不等式，证明了在适当条件下ADMM的局部收敛性和线性收敛速率，为实际应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the local convergence of the standard ADMM scheme for a class of nonconvex composite problems arising from modern imaging and machine learning models. This problem is constrained by a closed convex set, while its objective is the sum of a continuously differentiable (possibly nonconvex) smooth term and a polyhedral convex nonsmooth term composed with a linear mapping. Our analysis is mainly motivated by the recent works of Rockafellar [29,30]. We begin with an elementary proof of a key local strong convexity property of the Moreau envelope of polyhedral convex functions. Building on this property, we show that the strong variational sufficiency condition holds for the considered problem under appropriate assumptions. Using the strong variational sufficiency condition, we further derive a descent inequality for the ADMM iterates, in a form analogous to the classical descent analysis of ADMM for convex problems. As a consequence, for a suitable choice of the penalty parameter, we establish local convergence of the ADMM scheme to a primal-dual solution, and a local linear convergence rate for the case where the constraint set is polyhedral convex. Finally, we present three analytic examples to illustrate the applicability of our local convergence result and the necessity of the local assumptions.

</details>


### [100] [A reliability-aware randomized simheuristic for the team orienteering problem with stochastic travel times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20766)
*Michele Circelli*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种可靠性感知的模拟启发式算法，用于解决具有不确定旅行时间和全有或全无奖励策略的随机团队定向问题，通过结合启发式构造、随机化、局部搜索和蒙特卡洛模拟，在不确定性下直接评估候选解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究随机团队定向问题（TOP）中旅行时间不确定性和全有或无奖励策略的挑战，其中路线奖励在旅行时间超过可用预算时会完全丧失。这种设置使得期望奖励与路线可靠性之间的权衡成为解决方案设计的核心问题。

Method: 提出一种可靠性感知的模拟启发式算法，结合了基于节约法的构造启发式、受控随机化、局部搜索和蒙特卡洛模拟。该方法直接在不确定性下评估候选解，并使用估计的期望奖励和可靠性标准来选择解，而不是依赖确定性优化后进行事后随机评估。

Result: 在TOP文献基准实例上的计算实验表明，所提方法相对于在不确定性下评估的确定性基线显著提高了随机性能。在大多数实例中，模拟启发式算法同时增加了期望奖励和可靠性，在最宽松的情况下可靠性可接近0.99，同时保持适度的计算时间。

Conclusion: 可靠性感知的模拟启发式方法能有效处理随机团队定向问题中的不确定性，在期望奖励和可靠性之间取得良好平衡，为具有不确定旅行时间和全有或全无奖励策略的优化问题提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: We study a stochastic variant of the Team Orienteering Problem (TOP) with uncertain travel times and an all-or-nothing reward policy, under which the reward of a route is lost if its travel time exceeds the available budget. This setting makes the trade-off between expected reward and route reliability a central issue in solution design.
  To address this problem, we propose a reliability-aware simheuristic that combines a savings-based constructive heuristic, controlled randomization, local search, and Monte Carlo simulation. The method evaluates candidate solutions directly under uncertainty and selects them using both estimated expected reward and a reliability criterion, rather than relying on deterministic optimization followed by ex-post stochastic evaluation.
  Computational experiments on benchmark instances adapted from the TOP literature show that the proposed approach substantially improves stochastic performance with respect to a deterministic baseline evaluated under uncertainty. In most instances, the simheuristic increases both expected reward and reliability, and in the loosest regimes reliability can approach 0.99 while keeping computation times moderate.

</details>


### [101] [On hyperbolic PDEs, filtered feedback control laws, and fractal-like stability crossing curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20877)
*Wim Michiels,Federico Bribiesca-Argomedo,Jean Auriol*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究一类双曲型偏微分方程的边界控制问题，通过积分-差分方程等价表示，采用低通滤波器解决直接补偿反射项导致的稳定性脆弱问题，分析滤波器截止频率对鲁棒性和性能的平衡，确定最大稳定区间并推导模型参数失配下的稳定性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双曲型PDE边界控制中，直接补偿反射项会导致闭环系统缺乏强稳定性的脆弱问题。需要设计控制策略在保证鲁棒性的同时维持性能，特别是在存在模型参数失配的情况下。

Method: 1. 将双曲型PDE转化为积分-差分方程等价表示；2. 在控制律中加入低通滤波器解决稳定性问题；3. 确定滤波器截止频率倒数T的最大稳定区间；4. 考虑PDE参数模型失配，推导稳定性充分条件；5. 分析特殊情况下（所有失配集中在一个参数）的精确稳定图。

Result: 1. 确定了参数T的最大稳定区间；2. 推导了允许失配与截止频率关系的稳定性充分条件，在组合参数空间中形成以T=0为中心的锥形稳定区域；3. 在特殊情况下验证了稳定性图的定性行为，发现稳定性穿越曲线呈现分形结构；4. 用离散时滞极限系统解释了分形结构。

Conclusion: 低通滤波器能有效解决双曲型PDE边界控制的稳定性脆弱问题，滤波器截止频率的选择需要在高频鲁棒性和低频性能之间权衡。模型参数失配下的稳定性条件在参数空间中形成锥形区域。稳定性穿越曲线的分形结构揭示了系统动态的复杂性，可用离散时滞系统解释。

Abstract: The paper addresses the boundary control of a class of hyperbolic PDEs, based on an equivalent representation in terms of an integral-difference equation. The situation is considered where direct compensation of reflection terms induces a fragile closed-loop system, in the sense of lack of strong stability. This is theoretically resolved by adding a low-pass filter to the control law, but the choice of its cut-off frequency is crucial in balancing robustness at high frequencies and performance at low frequencies. First, the maximum stability interval in parameter $T$ is determined, with $T$ the inverse of the filter's cutoff frequency. Next, model mismatch on the PDE parameters is considered and a sufficient stability condition is derived in terms of allowable mismatch and cut-off frequency, satisfied in a region in the combined parameter space with a conic shape around $T=0$. Finally, this qualitative behavior is confirmed by exact stability charts for a special case where all model mismatch is contained into one parameter. It is highlighted that the set of stability crossing curves exhibits a fractal-like structure, which is explained using a limit system with discrete delays.

</details>


### [102] [Unfolding with a Wasserstein Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20903)
*Katy Craig,Benjamin Faktor,Benjamin Nachman*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出使用Wasserstein损失替代传统Richardson-Lucy去卷积中的KL散度，解决了数据展开问题，特别是在支持集不重叠时避免了分箱引入的数值误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Richardson-Lucy去卷积使用KL散度，要求测量数据与噪声模型输出的支持集重叠，通常通过分箱强制实现，这会引入数值误差。需要一种更稳健的方法来处理支持集不重叠的情况。

Method: 采用Wasserstein损失替代KL散度，建立优化器存在性和唯一性的严格条件，开发可证明收敛的广义Sinkhorn算法，仅需噪声模型和测量数据的经验观测，计算复杂度与数据规模而非环境维度相关。

Result: 建立了存在性和唯一性的充分必要条件，回答了Li等人的开放问题。算法在粒子物理中喷注质量展开的一维和二维问题上表现出色，相比传统RL去卷积，在分箱伪影显著时提供更稳健、准确的性能。

Conclusion: 最优传输方法为数据展开提供了稳健的替代方案，特别适用于支持集不重叠或分箱伪影显著的情况，在物理应用中有实际价值。

Abstract: Data unfolding -- the removal of noise or artifacts from measurements -- is a fundamental task across the experimental sciences. Of particular interest are applications in physics, where the dominant approach is Richardson-Lucy (RL) deconvolution. The classical RL approach aims to find denoised data that, once passed through the noise model, is as close as possible to the measured data in terms of Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence. This requires that the support of the measured data overlaps with the output of the noise model, a hypothesis typically enforced by binning, which introduces numerical error.
  As a counterpoint, the present work studies an alternative formulation using a Wasserstein loss. We establish sharp conditions for existence and uniqueness of optimizers, answering open questions of Li, et al., regarding necessary conditions for existence and uniqueness in the case of transport map noise models. We then develop a provably convergent generalized Sinkhorn algorithm to compute approximate optimizers. Our algorithm requires only empirical observations of the noise model and measured data and scales with the size of the data, rather than the ambient dimension. Numerical experiments on one- and two-dimensional problems inspired by jet mass unfolding in particle physics demonstrate that the optimal transport approach offers robust, accurate performance compared to classical RL deconvolution, particularly when binning artifacts are significant.

</details>


### [103] [Mirror-Free Proximal Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20918)
*Abhijeet Vyas,Brian Bullins*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需镜像映射的镜像近端算法（MFMP），扩展了经典方法，在相对光滑性和相对Lipschitz条件下分析收敛性，并展示了其在二阶和三阶光滑极小极大优化中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典镜像近端算法需要显式的镜像映射，这限制了其应用范围。作者旨在开发一种无需镜像映射的算法，同时保持经典方法的收敛性质，并能够处理更一般的优化问题。

Method: 提出镜像自由镜像近端算法（MFMP），使用更一般的势能算子替代标准Bregman散度，引入相对光滑性和相对Lipschitz性概念，并开发强单调变体来处理正则化泰勒展开子问题。

Result: 在相对光滑性和相对Lipschitz条件下证明了MFMP算法的收敛性，展示了强单调变体能够有效求解二阶和三阶光滑极小极大优化中的正则化泰勒展开子问题。

Conclusion: MFMP算法成功扩展了经典镜像近端方法，消除了对显式镜像映射的需求，为处理更广泛的优化问题提供了新工具，特别是在高阶光滑极小极大优化领域具有应用价值。

Abstract: We present a \emph{mirror-free} mirror prox (MFMP) algorithm, which extends the classic approach of Nemirovski (2004) to allow for proximal-like updates without the explicit need for a mirror map. We further analyze the convergence of our method under suitable notions of relative smoothness and relative Lipschitzness, for which we introduce a relaxation of the standard Bregman divergence in terms of more general potential operators. Finally, we show how a strongly monotone variant of our method allows us to solve regularized Taylor-expansion subproblems that appear in both second- and third-order smooth min-max optimization.

</details>


### [104] [Periodic solutions of nonlinear control systems with switching: a Lie-algebraic and contraction approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20935)
*Alexander Zuyev,Peter Benner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文分析具有bang-bang控制的非线性仿射系统的周期解，通过BCHD公式将周期解构造问题转化为指数映射复合的不动点问题，并应用于化学反应优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有bang-bang控制的非线性仿射系统的周期解问题，这类问题在具有约束输入的周期最优控制中自然出现，特别是在化学反应性能优化中有重要应用。

Method: 将周期解构造问题转化为指数映射复合的不动点问题，利用Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff-Dynkin公式处理；建立原始控制系统周期解与涉及迭代李括号的关联自治系统周期解的等价性；在平面情况下，应用Bendixson-Dulac定理将问题进一步简化为寻找该自治系统的平衡点。

Result: 建立了具有bang-bang控制的非线性仿射系统周期解的理论框架，证明了原始系统与关联自治系统周期解的等价性，在平面情况下提供了简化的平衡点分析方法。

Conclusion: 所发展的理论成功应用于具有约束控制的非线性化学反应模型，为这类系统的周期解分析和优化提供了有效的数学工具。

Abstract: This paper is devoted to the analysis of periodic solutions of nonlinear control-affine systems with bang-bang controls. Such problems naturally arise in periodic optimal control with constrained inputs, which have, in particular, important applications in the performance optimization of chemical reactions. We reduce the problem of constructing a periodic solution to that of finding a fixed point of a composition of exponential maps. The latter problem is then addressed using the Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff-Dynkin (BCHD) formula. We establish the equivalence between periodic solutions of the original control system and those of an associated autonomous system involving iterated Lie brackets. In the planar case, applying the Bendixson-Dulac theorem allows us to further simplify the problem to finding the equilibria of this autonomous system. The developed theory is then applied to nonlinear chemical reaction models with constrained controls.

</details>


### [105] [Mean field control with stopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21204)
*Pierre Cardaliaguet,Joe Jackson,Panagiotis E. Souganidis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究高维随机优化问题，结合控制和停止机制，通过均值场极限得到带跳跃动力学的控制问题，建立拟变分不等式理论框架并证明收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究同时包含控制和停止机制的高维随机优化问题，特别是中心规划者如何同时控制大量粒子群体并在任意时刻通过支付惩罚移除粒子，这类问题在金融、资源分配等领域有广泛应用。

Method: 采用均值场极限方法，将N粒子问题转化为带跳跃动力学的均值场控制问题；建立非线性障碍问题层次结构描述N粒子问题值函数；引入拟变分不等式（QVI）的粘性解概念，获得比较原理；结合正则性估计证明解的唯一性和收敛性。

Result: 证明了均值场值函数是QVI的唯一粘性解；建立了N粒子值函数向均值场值函数的收敛性；获得了比较原理和各种正则性估计，为这类带跳跃动力学的控制问题提供了完整的理论框架。

Conclusion: 成功建立了同时包含控制和停止机制的高维随机优化问题的均值场理论框架，通过拟变分不等式方法解决了带跳跃动力学的控制问题，为大规模粒子系统的优化控制提供了有效的分析工具。

Abstract: We study a high-dimensional stochastic optimization problem which features both control and stopping. In particular, a central planner steers a large population of particles, and can also remove particles at any time by paying a penalty. In the limit, we obtain a mean field control problem with discontinuous dynamics, in the sense that the controlled Fokker-Planck equation can have jumps. The value of the N-particle problem is characterized by a hierarchy of non-linear obstacle problems. The value of the limiting problem, meanwhile, solves an infinite-dimensional quasi-variational inequality (QVI). We introduce a notion of viscosity solution for this QVI, and obtain a comparison principle. Together with various regularity estimates, this comparison principle allows us to characterize the mean field value function as the unique viscosity solution of the QVI, and to establish the convergence of the N-particle value functions to the mean field value function.

</details>


### [106] [Two-stage robust bilevel optimization model for facility location considering operational service level under disruption risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21225)
*Haitao Hu,Jing Li,Jiafu Tang,Bo Zeng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对不确定环境下的双层设施选址问题，提出了一种新的两阶段鲁棒优化模型，并开发了改进的列与约束生成算法，显著提升了精确求解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于场景的随机方法在处理双层设施选址问题时，常因数据不足或场景数量指数增长而变得不切实际。为应对这一挑战，需要开发更有效的鲁棒优化方法来处理决策依赖的不确定性。

Method: 采用鲁棒优化方法，提出新颖的两阶段鲁棒双层设施选址模型。推导了多个结构性质以提升理论理解和求解效率，并基于此开发了增强的列与约束生成算法，专门用于处理决策依赖不确定性的鲁棒双层优化问题。

Result: 数值结果表明，与集中式两阶段鲁棒优化模型相比，新模型更关注需求满足，通常带来更高的服务效率和更好的供应能力利用率。在小规模中断下，新模型提供更好的服务性能；但在大规模中断下，集中式模型表现更有效。

Conclusion: 提出的两阶段鲁棒双层设施选址模型和增强算法有效解决了传统方法的局限性，在不同中断规模下展现出不同的性能优势，为分散式工业系统的鲁棒设计提供了新工具。

Abstract: The bilevel facility location problem (BO-FLP) is one of the core optimization problems behind the design of many decentralized industrial systems, e.g., supply chain systems where a supplier constructs some critical facilities and then uses them to serve retailers in a cost-effective fashion, while retailers directly handle customers aiming to minimize the total unmet demand in a rather independent fashion. When uncertainty is considered, scenario-based stochastic approaches are commonly used, but they often become impractical due to insufficient data or an exponential number of scenarios. To address this issue, this paper adopts robust optimization and proposes a novel two-stage robust bilevel facility location model. Several structural properties are derived to improve both theoretical understanding and solution efficiency. Based on this, an enhanced column-and-constraint generation algorithm is developed for robust bilevel optimization with decision-dependent uncertainty, significantly improving exact solution capability over the standard method. Numerical results show that, compared to the centralized two-stage RO model, our model pays more attention to demand fulfillment, typically resulting in higher service efficiency and better utilization of supply capacity. Under a small-scale disruption, this new model delivers better service performance. However, under a large-scale disruption, the centralized model performs more effectively.

</details>


### [107] [A Modular Approach to Stochastic Optimisation for Inverse Problems Using the Core Imaging Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21230)
*Evangelos Papoutsellis,Margaret A. G. Duff,Jakob S. Jørgensen,Sam Porter,Claire Delplancke,Gemma Fardell,Edoardo Pasca,Kris Thielemans*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: CIL扩展了随机优化模块，为成像逆问题提供模块化框架，支持灵活组合算法组件，适用于大规模CT和PET重建等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CIL优化模块主要针对确定性优化，缺乏对随机优化方法的系统支持。随机优化在大规模成像逆问题中具有显著计算优势，需要模块化框架来灵活配置算法组件，避免重复实现固定算法。

Method: 开发了模块化随机优化框架，提供可组合的构建块（如采样策略、步长策略、优化器组件），而非固定的黑盒算法实现。支持用户灵活组合这些组件来创建各种随机优化算法实例。

Result: 成功扩展了CIL的优化模块，实现了可扩展的随机优化框架。在真实世界数据集（X射线CT和PET重建）上的实验证明了框架的实用性和灵活性，能够有效处理大规模成像逆问题。

Conclusion: 该扩展为研究社区提供了强大、可扩展的随机优化套件，支持开发、测试和基准测试逆问题的随机方法，特别适用于大规模成像应用。

Abstract: The Core Imaging Library (CIL) is an open-source versatile Python framework for solving inverse problems with special emphasis on imaging applications such as computed tomography (CT), using a plug-in architecture for data and operators, interfacing to toolboxes such as ASTRA, TIGRE and SIRF. A key component of CIL is its optimisation module enabling users to flexibly combine mathematical operators and functionals to form smooth and non-smooth optimisation problems and solve these with a range of first-order algorithms. The present work introduces an expansion of CIL with a new modular framework for stochastic optimisation, allowing researchers to easily use a variety of existing stochastic optimisation algorithms as well form new ones by combining modular building blocks. Users can flexibly configure algorithmic components, adapt to diverse problem structures, and experiment with various sampling and step size strategies. Rather than individual black-box implementations of each fixed algorithm with significant redundancies, our design is modular providing building blocks that can be flexibly combined to realise a wealth of algorithm instances. The framework is particularly well-suited for large-scale applications, where stochastic methods offer notable computational advantages over deterministic approaches. To demonstrate its versatility and practical utility, we present experiments on real-world datasets from imaging inverse problems, such as X-Ray CT and Positron Emission Tomography (PET) reconstruction. In summary, the presented software expansion aims to support the research community with a robust, extensible optimisation suite for developing, testing, and benchmarking stochastic methods for inverse problems.

</details>


### [108] [Theorem of Alternative for Extended Homogeneous Linear System and its Application in Conic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21500)
*Yurii Nesterov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Gordan择一定理构造锥优化不可行启动原始对偶方法的新框架，通过最小化带线性等式约束的障碍函数，将锥优化问题转化为无约束优化问题，并分析了阻尼牛顿法和短步路径跟踪法的多项式时间复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统锥优化方法通常需要可行启动点，限制了实际应用。本文旨在开发一种不可行启动的原始对偶方法，能够从任意初始点开始，同时保证多项式时间收敛性，并充分利用热启动机会。

Method: 基于Gordan择一定理，将锥优化问题转化为最小化带线性等式约束的障碍函数的辅助凸问题。该辅助问题的解可以轻松转换为原始问题的解。采用两种优化方案：阻尼牛顿法和短步路径跟踪法，均能实现多项式时间收敛。

Result: 证明了两种方法（阻尼牛顿法和短步路径跟踪法）都具有多项式时间复杂性。方法能够从任意初始点开始（不可行启动），同时保证对偶间隙达到目标精度ε。特别地，能够确保原始和对偶问题中线性等式约束的残差达到机器精度水平，与输入参数ε无关。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于Gordan择一定理的统一框架，用于构造不可行启动的原始对偶锥优化方法。该方法具有多项式时间收敛性，能够处理任意初始点，充分利用热启动机会，并在数值精度方面具有优势（约束残差可达机器精度）。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a new framework for constructing infeasible-start primal-dual methods for Conic Optimization. Our approach can be seen as an unambiguous consequence of Gordan Theorem of Alternative. Given by the target upper bound $ε> 0$ for the duality gap as the only input parameter, we form an auxiliary convex problem of minimizing barrier function with linear equality constraints. Its solution can be easily transformed to the requested output. This function can be minimized by different schemes of Unconstrained Optimization, with possible quadratic convergence in the end of the process. In our paper, we analyze the Damped Newton Method and a short-step path-following scheme. For both of them, we prove polynomial-time complexity results. Our methods are able to benefit from the hot-start opportunities. We can ensure the residual of the linear equality constraints in the primal and dual problems to be on the level of machine accuracy, independently on the input parameter $ε$.

</details>


### [109] [Optimal local linear convergence of Nesterov's accelerated gradient method for $C^2$ functions under the Polyak--Łojasiewicz inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21516)
*Zixu Feng,Hao Yuan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 证明了Nesterov加速梯度法在满足Polyak-Łojasiewicz不等式的C²函数上达到最优局部线性收敛率，无需高阶光滑性或额外几何正则性假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分析通常需要高阶光滑性或局部极小值流形的额外几何正则性假设来获得最优收敛率。本文旨在在更弱的C²光滑性假设下，为Nesterov加速梯度法建立最优局部线性收敛率。

Method: 采用两阶段论证：首先建立粗略但有效的局部线性收敛率，然后基于此先验收敛保证，获得线性化迭代算子的精细刻画，从而得到最优收敛率。分析框架也适用于连续时间Heavy Ball动力学。

Result: 证明了Nesterov加速梯度法在满足Polyak-Łojasiewicz不等式的C²函数上达到最优局部线性收敛率ρ = (√(3L+μ) - 2√μ)/(√(3L+μ)) + ε，其中ε为任意小常数。在相同条件下恢复了Heavy Ball动力学的最优局部指数收敛率√μ。

Conclusion: 该工作显著降低了获得最优收敛率所需的光滑性假设，仅需C²光滑性而非传统的高阶光滑性。两阶段分析框架具有普适性，数值实验验证了理论结果。

Abstract: In this work, we establish that Nesterov's accelerated gradient method, applied to $C^2$ functions satisfying the Polyak--Łojasiewicz inequality around local minimizers, achieves the optimal local linear convergence rate $ρ=\frac{\sqrt{3L+μ}-2\sqrtμ}{\sqrt{3L+μ}}+\varepsilon$, where $\varepsilon$ is an arbitrarily small constant. Our analysis requires neither higher-order smoothness beyond $C^2$ of the objective function nor any additional geometric regularity of the submanifold of local minimizers. The key novelty lies in a two-stage argument: we first establish a coarse yet valid local linear convergence rate and then, building upon this a priori convergence guarantee, obtain a refined characterization of the linearized iteration operator, which yields the optimal rate. As a result, we only need to slightly strengthen the standard $C^{1,1}$ assumption, which is commonly required in theoretical analyses of linear convergence for first-order methods, to $C^2$ smoothness. Moreover, the same analytical framework allows us to recover, under identical conditions, the optimal local exponential convergence rate $\sqrtμ$ for the continuous-time Heavy Ball dynamics. Finally, a representative numerical experiment corroborates our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [110] [Sinkhorn algorithms for entropic vector quantile regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21554)
*Kengo Kato,Boyu Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种用于向量分位数回归的Sinkhorn型算法，建立了线性收敛性，并推导了显式定量界


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量分位数回归是基于最优传输的框架，将线性分位数回归扩展到向量值响应变量。现有方法在计算上存在挑战，需要开发更实用的算法并建立收敛性理论。

Method: 提出了两种Sinkhorn型算法：1）基于求解完整Schrödinger型系统的经典Sinkhorn迭代的直接适应；2）新的投影梯度步长算法，用显式更新替换隐式更新，计算上更实用。

Result: 对于紧支撑边际分布，两种算法在双重目标值和迭代序列上都建立了线性收敛性。关键创新是推导了对偶势和Sinkhorn迭代的显式定量界。

Conclusion: 本文为向量分位数回归开发了两种计算上可行的Sinkhorn型算法，并提供了严格的收敛性分析和定量界，为实际应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Vector quantile regression (VQR) is an optimal transport (OT)-based framework that extends linear quantile regression to vector-valued response variables and can be formulated as an OT problem with a mean-independence constraint. In this paper, we study two Sinkhorn-type algorithms for VQR with entropic regularization, building on our previous work on its duality theory. The first is a direct adaptation of the classical Sinkhorn iteration based on solving the full Schrödinger-type system characterizing the dual potentials, which requires solving an implicit functional equation at each iteration. The second algorithm, which is new in the literature, replaces the implicit update with a projected gradient step, resulting in a modified scheme that is computationally more practical. For both algorithms, and for general compactly supported marginals, we establish linear convergence in both the dual objective value and the iterates. A key innovation in our analysis is the derivation of explicit quantitative bounds on the dual potentials and Sinkhorn iterates.

</details>


### [111] [Compressed Distributed Stochastic Nonconvex Optimization with Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21640)
*Antai Xie,Xiaoqiang Ren,Xinlei Yi,Tao Yang,Xiaofan Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种压缩分布式随机梯度下降算法，解决具有压缩通信和差分隐私的分布式随机非凸优化问题，在一般压缩算子下保持鲁棒性，并实现线性加速收敛和隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式优化中通信成本高且存在隐私泄露风险，需要设计既能压缩通信又能保护隐私的高效算法，同时保证收敛性能。

Method: 提出压缩分布式随机梯度下降算法，支持相对和绝对压缩误差的一般压缩算子，结合差分隐私机制，在非凸函数和P-Ł条件下分析收敛性。

Result: 算法在平滑非凸函数下达到一阶稳定点的线性加速收敛率O(1/√(nT))，在P-Ł条件下达到O(1/(nT^θ))，已知P-Ł常数时达到O(1/(nT))，并能实现(0,δ)-差分隐私而不牺牲收敛精度。

Conclusion: 所提算法有效解决了压缩通信和隐私保护的分布式优化问题，在多种条件下均能保证收敛性能，并通过数值实验验证了理论结果。

Abstract: This paper studies distributed stochastic nonconvex optimization problems with compressed communication and differential privacy, in which each agent aims to minimize the sum of all agents' cost functions by using local compressed information exchange. To this end, we propose a compressed distributed stochastic gradient descent algorithm, which is robust under a general class of compression operators that allow both relative and absolute compression errors. We then show that the proposed algorithm finds the first-order stationary point for smooth nonconvex functions with the linear speedup convergence rate $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{nT})$ and converges to the optimum if the global cost function additionally satisfies the Polyak--Łojasiewicz (P--Ł) condition with the convergence rate $\mathcal{O}(1/(nT^θ)),θ\in(0,1)$, where $T$ is the total number of iterations and $n$ is the number of agents. Furthermore, if the P--Ł~constant is known in advance, we show that the proposed algorithm achieves a convergence rate $\mathcal{O}(1/(nT))$. Finally, we show that the proposed algorithm is able to achieve $(0,δ)$-differential privacy without sacrificing convergence accuracy. Numerical experiments are carried out to

</details>


### [112] [Nonlinear Control Synchronization Method for Fractional-order Time Derivatives Chaotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21747)
*Vivek Mishra,S. K. Agrawal*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究分数阶时间导数系统的非线性控制同步技术，使用Adams-Bashforth-Moulton方法求解分数阶系统，验证了两个混沌系统同步的可靠性和适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同步现象在物理、工程、生物、经济等多个领域广泛存在，在神经科学和通信系统中具有重要应用。分数阶系统相比整数阶系统能更精确描述实际系统动态特性，因此研究分数阶混沌系统的同步控制具有重要意义。

Method: 采用非线性控制同步技术处理分数阶时间导数系统，使用Adams-Bashforth-Moulton数值方法求解分数阶微分方程，保持两个系统处于混沌状态的同时实现相互同步。

Result: 数值仿真验证了该方法对两个混沌系统同步的可靠性和易用性，重点分析了同步时间和导数重排的影响，这些是本文最重要的贡献部分。

Conclusion: 本文提出的分数阶混沌系统非线性控制同步方法有效可行，为分数阶系统同步控制提供了理论支持和数值验证，在工程应用中具有实用价值。

Abstract: "Synchronization of two dynamical systems" is the term used to describe the phenomenon when two or more systems gradually change their states or behaviors to become similar or identical. This can happen in a lot of fields, such as physics, engineering, biology, and economics. Synchronization finds applications in neurology and communication systems. It is present in both man-made and organic systems. The nonlinear control synchronization technique for fractional-order time derivative systems is described in this article, where the Adams Basford Moulton method is used for solving the fractional-order system. The reliability and ease of applicability for two chaotic systems are demonstrated by the numerical simulation. Furthermore, in this article, both systems were kept in a chaotic condition while being synchronized with each other. The effects of synchronizing time and rearranging the derivatives are the most significant sections of this article.

</details>


### [113] [Safe and wind-aware synchronous path planning for a fleet of fixed-wing constant speed aircraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21789)
*Maël Feurgard,Gautier Hattenberger,Nicolas Durand,Simon Lacroix*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Dubins路径枚举的集中式方法，解决固定翼无人机编队飞行模式转换的路径规划问题，确保同步到达、最小间隔和巡航速度保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机路径规划本身具有挑战性，特别是固定翼无人机编队在飞行模式转换（如编队间切换）时需要同时满足同步到达、最小间隔保持和巡航速度维持，这增加了问题的复杂性。

Method: 基于Dubins路径枚举的集中式方法：给定编队飞行时间，为每架飞机构建可能的路径集合；并行检查每对路径间的间隔；将结果转化为整数线性规划问题的系数，判断是否存在全编队无冲突解；通过递增分辨率采样不同飞行时间，直到满足用户定义的精度要求。

Result: 通过蒙特卡洛模拟对多达20架飞机同时进行基准测试，平均计算时间8秒内达到95%的成功率。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效解决固定翼无人机编队飞行模式转换的路径规划问题，在可接受的计算时间内实现高成功率，为复杂编队操作提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Path planning for multiple unmanned aerial vehicles is a difficult task, and even more for a fleet of fixed-wing aircraft. One specific case is the transition to, or between, formation flight patterns, which requires synchronous arrivals while ensuring minimal separation, and ideally maintaining cruise speed. We present a centralized method to solve this problem based on enumerating different Dubins paths. Given a travel time for the fleet, it builds a set of possible paths for each aircraft. Then, it checks in parallel separation between each path pair. This yields coefficients for an Integer Linear Programming problem determining if a fleet-wide conflict-free solution exists. This process is repeated for different travel times sampled with increasing resolution until the user-defined accuracy is met. The method is benchmarked with Monte-Carlo simulations considering up to 20 aircraft simultaneously, achieving an 95% success rate for an average 8 seconds computation time.

</details>


### [114] [Privacy-Preserving Formation Control for Networked Underactuated USVs: A Passivity-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21804)
*Jingyi Zhao,Wenxuan Wang,Weijun Zhou,Yongxin Wu,Yuhu Wu,Yann Le Gorrec*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种在严格隐私要求下的多无人水面艇协同轨迹规划与跟踪控制方法，通过仅共享估计的编队质心避免个体轨迹泄露，实现隐私保护下的编队控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统协同方法需要直接共享位置信息，存在隐私泄露风险。本文旨在解决多无人水面艇在严格隐私要求下的协同控制问题，避免个体轨迹信息暴露。

Method: 采用估计的编队质心作为唯一共享变量，基于此交互机制生成编队导向轨迹。使用端口哈密顿系统建模集体动力学，为每个USV设计基于无源性的跟踪控制器。

Result: 严格证明了闭环系统的稳定性，在真实USV平台上进行实验验证，实现了有效的编队跟踪和隐私保护。扩展并验证了文献[26]的方法，克服了其仅限于理想质点模型和缺乏反馈控制的局限。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法在严格隐私约束下实现了多无人水面艇的有效协同控制，通过仅共享编队质心信息保护个体隐私，实验验证了方法的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: This paper studies coordinated trajectory planning and tracking control for multiple unmanned surface vessels (USVs) under strict privacy requirements. To avoid the privacy risks associated with direct position sharing in conventional cooperative methods, the proposed approach adopts an estimated fleet centroid as the only shared variable, preventing individual trajectory disclosure while enabling coordination. Based on this interaction mechanism, a formation-oriented trajectory is generated for the fleet. The collective dynamics are modeled using Port-Hamiltonian systems, and a passivity-based tracking controller is designed for each USV to accurately follow the planned trajectories. The stability of the closed-loop system is rigorously proven, and experiments on a real USV platform confirm effective formation tracking and privacy preservation. The proposed result extends and validates through experimental results the approach in [26] that was limited to idealized pointmass models and lacked a feedback control.

</details>


### [115] [A Moser-Type Construction for the Liouville Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21850)
*Alfio Borzì,Marco Caponigro,Arianna Vicari*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 将Moser体积形式引理推广到动力学刘维尔方程，建立了相空间密度在单位时间内通过刘维尔方程连接的充要条件


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的Moser体积形式引理从静态情况扩展到动力学刘维尔方程，研究相空间密度在动力学演化下的连接性问题

Method: 通过速度边际的自然相容性条件，构造显式的力场族，该构造基于速度变量的加权椭圆问题

Result: 证明了两个光滑正相空间密度在单位时间内可通过刘维尔方程连接的充要条件是速度边际满足相容性条件，并提供了数值实验验证

Conclusion: 成功将Moser引理扩展到动力学刘维尔方程，建立了相空间密度连接性的完整理论框架和构造方法

Abstract: We present a novel extension of Moser's volume form lemma to the kinetic Liouville equation. In particular, we show that two smooth, positive phase-space densities $f$ and $g$ can be connected in unit time by the Liouville equation if and only if a natural compatibility condition on velocity marginals is satisfied. Under this condition, an explicit family of force fields is constructed via a weighted elliptic problem in the velocity variable. Results of numerical experiments are presented to validate the theoretical framework.

</details>


### [116] [Towards sparse optimization over convex loops: Equivalence of Square Root Velocity distance and Wasserstein-Fisher-Rao](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22009)
*Giacomo Cristinelli,José A. Iglesias*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文证明了对于平面上的凸绝对连续S¹浸入，SRVT诱导的形状距离等价于作用于其长度测度的WFR距离，并研究了WFR正则化形状优化中的稀疏性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然Wasserstein-Fisher-Rao距离在S²上与经典弹性形状距离一致，但这种对应关系在S¹上一般失效，且对于凸曲线无法从现有二维论证推导。本文旨在建立凸绝对连续S¹浸入的SRVT形状距离与WFR距离的等价性，并研究WFR正则化形状优化中的稀疏性。

Method: 利用圆通用覆盖上最优传输的单调性原理，证明存在达到SRVT下确界的最优重参数化，从而得到一维非平衡最优传输重构。进一步研究长度测度WFR正则化形状优化中的稀疏性，证明离散参考测度时线性优化的有限性，提出适合条件梯度算法的凸正齐次正则化器。

Result: 证明了平面凸绝对连续S¹浸入的SRVT形状距离等价于作用于其长度测度的WFR距离。建立了离散参考测度下WFR球线性优化的有限性结果，并设计了适合条件梯度算法的凸正齐次正则化器。

Conclusion: 本文建立了S¹凸曲线SRVT形状距离与WFR距离的等价性，填补了二维论证无法覆盖的空白。同时揭示了WFR正则化形状优化中的稀疏性特性，为相关算法设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The Wasserstein-Fisher-Rao (WFR) distance on $S^{2}$ has recently been shown to coincide with a classical elastic distance between $S^{2}$-immersions in the theory of Riemannian shape analysis. While this correspondence holds in dimension $2$, the analogous statement fails in general on $S^{1}$ and, in the case of convex curves, it cannot be derived from existing two-dimensional arguments. In this paper, we establish that for convex absolutely continuous immersions of $S^{1}$ in the plane, the shape distance induced by the square root velocity transformation (SRVT) is indeed equivalent to the WFR distance acting on their associated length measures. The proof exploits a monotonicity principle for optimal transport on the universal cover of the circle, which in turn guarantees the existence of an optimal reparametrization achieving the SRVT infimum and enables a one-dimensional unbalanced optimal transport reformulation. Motivated by this equivalence, we further investigate the role of sparsity in shape optimization problems formulated in terms of length measures and regularized by the WFR distance. We study linear optimization over the corresponding balls, for which we prove a finiteness result when the reference measure is discrete, and propose a convex, positively one-homogeneous regularizer suitable for conditional gradient algorithms.

</details>


### [117] [Here, there and everywhere: state-dependent time-inconsistent stochastic control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22022)
*Dylan Possamaï,Mateo Rodriguez Polo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究了连续时间框架下的时间不一致随机控制问题，提出了基于倒向随机微分方程(BSDEs)的概率表示方法，并在线性二次调节器问题中进行了应用验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间不一致随机控制问题在连续时间框架下具有挑战性，特别是当当前状态影响目标函数时会导致不一致性。需要建立一种概率表示方法来刻画这类问题的均衡值函数。

Method: 采用倒向随机微分方程(BSDEs)系统作为概率表示工具，通过BSDEs系统来封装解决时间不一致控制问题所需的均衡值函数。

Result: 成功建立了时间不一致随机控制问题的BSDEs概率表示框架，该框架能够处理当前状态影响目标函数导致的不一致情况，并在时间不一致的线性二次调节器问题中验证了理论的有效性。

Conclusion: BSDEs系统为连续时间框架下的时间不一致随机控制问题提供了有效的概率表示方法，在线性二次调节器等具体应用中展示了理论的实用价值。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of time-inconsistent stochastic control within a continuous-time framework. Its primary focus lies in uncovering a probabilistic representation, specifically in the shape of a system of backward stochastic differential equations (BSDEs). These equations encapsulate the equilibrium value function essential for resolving cases where the present state affecting the target functional triggers the inconsistency. Additionally, the paper offers an application exemplifying this theory through the time-inconsistent linear--quadratic regulator.

</details>


### [118] [Distributionally robust optimization for recommendation selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22090)
*Tomoya Yanagi,Shunnosuke Ikeda,Ken Kobayashi,Yuichi Takano*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于分布鲁棒优化的推荐系统框架，在保持准确率的同时提升推荐多样性，通过惩罚交替方向法高效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统虽然能达到高准确率，但缺乏多样性会影响用户满意度；基于金融投资组合理论的方法对评分统计估计误差敏感，需要更鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: 建立基于矩模糊集的分布鲁棒优化模型，设计惩罚交替方向法（PADM）高效求解，并证明其收敛性。

Result: 在三个公开评分数据集上的实验表明，DRO模型在保持相同准确率水平下能生成更多样化的推荐，且每个用户的求解仅需几秒钟。

Conclusion: 该研究建立的DRO框架有潜力提升各种协同过滤方法的推荐质量，实现了准确性与多样性的更好平衡。

Abstract: Recommender systems play an essential role in online services by providing personalized item lists to support users' decision-making processes. While collaborative filtering methods can achieve high accuracy, it is crucial to consider not only accuracy but also the diversity of recommended items to improve user satisfaction. Although financial portfolio theory has been applied to balance these factors, existing models are often sensitive to estimation errors in rating statistics. To overcome these challenges, we establish a computational framework of distributionally robust optimization (DRO) for recommendation selection. We first formulate a cardinality-constrained DRO model based on moment-based ambiguity sets to select a specified number of items for each user.
